<a id="L2922"></a>**L2922** `01:50:17` >> Gradually, perhaps even naturally, the

<a id="L2923"></a>**L2923** `01:50:19` signs of the cataclysm faded. The storm

<a id="L2924"></a>**L2924** `01:50:22` ceased. The fires died. The coming

<a id="L2925"></a>**L2925** `01:50:25` threat of a new apocalypse began to flee

<a id="L2926"></a>**L2926** `01:50:26` from sight. And with the imminent threat

<a id="L2927"></a>**L2927** `01:50:28` of destruction gone, the four factions,

<a id="L2928"></a>**L2928** `01:50:31` Viking, samurai knights, and Wuin, all

<a id="L2929"></a>**L2929** `01:50:34` had the opportunity to look around and

<a id="L2930"></a>**L2930** `01:50:36` see what had become of their war.

<a id="L2931"></a>**L2931** `01:50:38` Mindless slaughter with no end in sight.

<a id="L2932"></a>**L2932** `01:50:40` The animosity and hate began to dry up.

<a id="L2933"></a>**L2933** `01:50:42` And like raids of sun piercing through a

<a id="L2934"></a>**L2934** `01:50:44` cloudy sky, hope began to build in the

<a id="L2935"></a>**L2935** `01:50:47` hearts of the faction leaders as they

<a id="L2936"></a>**L2936** `01:50:49` lay down their arms. Ambassadors from

<a id="L2937"></a>**L2937** `01:50:51` all four factions gathered together in a

<a id="L2938"></a>**L2938** `01:50:53` sacred place known as Wyvernale, where a

<a id="L2939"></a>**L2939** `01:50:55` truce was declared. The truce of

<a id="L2940"></a>**L2940** `01:50:57` Weberale saw the four factions set aside

<a id="L2941"></a>**L2941** `01:50:59` their differences and come together in

<a id="L2942"></a>**L2942** `01:51:01` peace, camaraderie, and hopefully

<a id="L2943"></a>**L2943** `01:51:03` lasting kinship. Games were declared,

<a id="L2944"></a>**L2944** `01:51:06` celebrations to the end of the wars.

<a id="L2945"></a>**L2945** `01:51:08` Feasts were had and tears ran from the

<a id="L2946"></a>**L2946** `01:51:10` eyes of weary warriors and loved ones as

<a id="L2947"></a>**L2947** `01:51:12` a promise of peace was at last standing

<a id="L2948"></a>**L2948** `01:51:15` before them. However, beneath the

<a id="L2949"></a>**L2949** `01:51:17` surface, a cancer was growing, and none

<a id="L2950"></a>**L2950** `01:51:20` could foresee it until it was far too

<a id="L2951"></a>**L2951** `01:51:22` late. But at least for now,

<a id="L2952"></a>**L2952** `01:51:24` there was hope.

<a id="L2953"></a>**L2953** `01:51:30` Starting with the year 4 season 1, the

<a id="L2954"></a>**L2954** `01:51:31` game went into a new direction. We got

<a id="L2955"></a>**L2955** `01:51:33` new developers, new ideas, new plans for

<a id="L2956"></a>**L2956** `01:51:36` the future. From now on, there will be

<a id="L2957"></a>**L2957** `01:51:38` only two new hero each season. At season

<a id="L2958"></a>**L2958** `01:51:40` 2 and season 4, and the game will have a

<a id="L2959"></a>**L2959** `01:51:42` much bigger lore theme around it. We

<a id="L2960"></a>**L2960** `01:51:44` also got a lot of minor tweaks on

<a id="L2961"></a>**L2961** `01:51:46` heroes, but besides the removal of

<a id="L2962"></a>**L2962** `01:51:48` certain option selects, which a lot of

<a id="L2963"></a>**L2963** `01:51:50` competitive players long asked for,

<a id="L2964"></a>**L2964** `01:51:52` nothing groundbreaking changed.

<a id="L2965"></a>**L2965** `01:51:54` >> Just like every other live service game

<a id="L2966"></a>**L2966** `01:51:55` at the time, Fro introduced a battle

<a id="L2967"></a>**L2967** `01:51:57` pass with a free and a premium version

<a id="L2968"></a>**L2968** `01:51:59` with cosmetics and all yada yada. It did

<a id="L2969"></a>**L2969** `01:52:02` start the trend though of Ubisoft

<a id="L2970"></a>**L2970** `01:52:03` releasing a new emblem outline for every

<a id="L2971"></a>**L2971** `01:52:05` season because there are always two of

<a id="L2972"></a>**L2972** `01:52:07` them.

<a id="L2973"></a>**L2973** `01:52:07` >> We also saw the return of Carousel of

<a id="L2974"></a>**L2974** `01:52:09` Death and a new crossover event with

<a id="L2975"></a>**L2975** `01:52:12` Prince of Persia.

<a id="L2976"></a>**L2976** `01:52:13` >> This event was another one of my

<a id="L2977"></a>**L2977** `01:52:15` favorites. Prince of Persia event was 10

<a id="L2978"></a>**L2978** `01:52:16` out of 10 down that harbor elevator on

<a id="L2979"></a>**L2979** `01:52:19` uh a point. It was replaced with moving

<a id="L2980"></a>**L2980** `01:52:22` death wheels that are uh wall-mounted.

<a id="L2981"></a>**L2981** `01:52:25` But uh there was a rogue NPC. He would

<a id="L2982"></a>**L2982** `01:52:28` uh literally just beat the [ __ ] out of

<a id="L2983"></a>**L2983** `01:52:31` you. He had that time dagger manipulated

<a id="L2984"></a>**L2984** `01:52:34` time in a way. Once you killed the NPC

<a id="L2985"></a>**L2985** `01:52:36` and picked up the dagger, it was just

<a id="L2986"></a>**L2986** `01:52:38` absolute mayhem. I think you could make

<a id="L2987"></a>**L2987** `01:52:41` like a giant AoE explosion. It would

<a id="L2988"></a>**L2988** `01:52:44` just one hit you. Absolutely insane.

<a id="L2989"></a>**L2989** `01:52:50` I think when it comes to like specific

<a id="L2990"></a>**L2990** `01:52:52` seasons, the first year of this game was

<a id="L2991"></a>**L2991** `01:52:54` like the best. like all of the like

<a id="L2994"></a>**L2994** `01:53:01` customization, like the scream mask for

<a id="L3009"></a>**L3009** `01:53:35` since I'd say Marching Fire that's

<a id="L3024"></a>**L3024** `01:54:12` Not all were joyous and excited for the

<a id="L3025"></a>**L3025** `01:54:14` proposed piece. There were some who felt

<a id="L3026"></a>**L3026** `01:54:16` it was a bitter poison to the mouth, an

<a id="L3027"></a>**L3027** `01:54:18` insult to history and time and to human

<a id="L3028"></a>**L3028** `01:54:21` nature. For one night, Astria, the truce

<a id="L3029"></a>**L3029** `01:54:24` was a step too far for her. A devout

<a id="L3030"></a>**L3030** `01:54:27` follower of Apolon's ideals, Astria

<a id="L3031"></a>**L3031** `01:54:29` believed that she had been given a

<a id="L3032"></a>**L3032** `01:54:30` vision from the god Horcos to follow up

<a id="L3033"></a>**L3033** `01:54:33` and finish Apolon's work. She sought out

<a id="L3034"></a>**L3034** `01:54:36` the remnants of the Blackstone Legion,

<a id="L3035"></a>**L3035** `01:54:38` the destitute and all but forgotten

<a id="L3036"></a>**L3036** `01:54:39` warriors who had been shamed and tossed

<a id="L3037"></a>**L3037** `01:54:41` aside. She also sought out the

<a id="L3038"></a>**L3038** `01:54:43` harbingers like Vortiger, Hula, Sakura,

<a id="L3039"></a>**L3039** `01:54:45` and Sunda, all of whom felt that this

<a id="L3040"></a>**L3040** `01:54:47` peace would force them back into the

<a id="L3041"></a>**L3041** `01:54:49` shadows of obscurity. Through these

<a id="L3042"></a>**L3042** `01:54:51` forces, she forged a new power, the

<a id="L3043"></a>**L3043** `01:54:54` Order of Horos, a military body with the

<a id="L3044"></a>**L3044** `01:54:56` goal of ending the truths and ending

<a id="L3045"></a>**L3045** `01:54:58` these lies of peace. And to fulfill

<a id="L3046"></a>**L3046** `01:55:01` this, Astra used the secret weapon she'd

<a id="L3047"></a>**L3047** `01:55:03` had Sundai help her create, Draconite

<a id="L3048"></a>**L3048** `01:55:06` corruption. Through the use of a

<a id="L3049"></a>**L3049** `01:55:07` dangerous and unstable mineral called

<a id="L3050"></a>**L3050** `01:55:09` Draconite, the Order of Horcos launch

<a id="L3051"></a>**L3051** `01:55:11` attacks across Eithore, spreading a

<a id="L3052"></a>**L3052** `01:55:13` horrid plague of corruption and killing

<a id="L3053"></a>**L3053** `01:55:15` thousands, all in the name of ending a

<a id="L3054"></a>**L3054** `01:55:18` supposed peace and start an age of

<a id="L3055"></a>**L3055** `01:55:22` tyranny.

<a id="L3631"></a>**L3631** `02:17:03` >> The Italian world of FH was an ecosystem

<a id="L3635"></a>**L3635** `02:17:13` It was held together by a single nexus

<a id="L3636"></a>**L3636** `02:17:15` which hosted the art of the Italian

<a id="L3637"></a>**L3637** `02:17:17` collective the faced community named

<a id="L3638"></a>**L3638** `02:17:20` Italy for honor that held the

<a id="L3639"></a>**L3639** `02:17:22` regulations for the custom game worlds

<a id="L3640"></a>**L3640** `02:17:24` as it serves as a database regarding all

<a id="L3641"></a>**L3641** `02:17:26` the events happening within it. It was

<a id="L3642"></a>**L3642** `02:17:28` kept in check by several admins which

<a id="L3643"></a>**L3643** `02:17:31` changed over time and it even had this

<a id="L3644"></a>**L3644** `02:17:33` cooks and memes and thanks to it

<a id="L3645"></a>**L3645** `02:17:36` friendships were created, recruits were

<a id="L3646"></a>**L3646** `02:17:38` gathered and clans were formed. Fight

<a id="L3647"></a>**L3647** `02:17:41` Clubs, team-based. The single custom

<a id="L3648"></a>**L3648** `02:17:43` game always has been the foundation of

<a id="L3649"></a>**L3649** `02:17:45` the Italian format of tournaments, which

<a id="L3650"></a>**L3650** `02:17:47` brought so much player to Clan Wars, but

<a id="L3651"></a>**L3651** `02:17:49` got progressively abandoned in favor of

<a id="L3652"></a>**L3652** `02:17:51` the international dominion based ones

<a id="L3653"></a>**L3653** `02:17:54` since this video is nothing short of

<a id="L3654"></a>**L3654** `02:17:56` Martian to the FH comp players in the

<a id="L3655"></a>**L3655** `02:17:58` rest of the world. Kings Herobase. This

<a id="L3656"></a>**L3656** `02:18:01` is an Italian exclusive spin of season

<a id="L3657"></a>**L3657** `02:18:04` number one series. Rather than using

<a id="L3658"></a>**L3658** `02:18:07` community based feedback for predicting

<a id="L3660"></a>**L3660** `02:18:10` a hero, the Italian system simply uses

<a id="L3661"></a>**L3661** `02:18:13` democratic hero mirrors with no prior

<a id="L3662"></a>**L3662** `02:18:15` requirements. It was common to make a

<a id="L3664"></a>**L3664** `02:18:19` challenging the king for then posting

<a id="L3900"></a>**L3900** `02:27:02` There are four sacred relics in

<a id="L3901"></a>**L3901** `02:27:03` Heathmore that the pirates seek. And the

<a id="L3902"></a>**L3902** `02:27:06` first draws its origins to Heathmore's

<a id="L3903"></a>**L3903** `02:27:08` ancient past. Long ago, legends speak of

<a id="L3904"></a>**L3904** `02:27:11` Lord Ramiel, the first warden, who swore

<a id="L3905"></a>**L3905** `02:27:14` an oath to the wvens, the once great

<a id="L3906"></a>**L3906** `02:27:16` guardians of Heathmore's wilds. By

<a id="L3907"></a>**L3907** `02:27:19` drinking the wven blood stored in the

<a id="L3908"></a>**L3908** `02:27:21` chalice of immortality, Ramiel gained

<a id="L3909"></a>**L3909** `02:27:23` immortality himself, and through it, he

<a id="L3910"></a>**L3910** `02:27:26` was able to protect and defend Heathmore

<a id="L3911"></a>**L3911** `02:27:28` from tyrants and monsters. However,

<a id="L3912"></a>**L3912** `02:27:30` Ramiel and the Wyverns would meet their

<a id="L3913"></a>**L3913** `02:27:32` end at the start of the cataclysm, when

<a id="L3914"></a>**L3914** `02:27:35` calamity and destruction fell upon them

<a id="L3915"></a>**L3915** `02:27:37` all. This legend would be told by

<a id="L3916"></a>**L3916** `02:27:40` countless peoples and cultures across

<a id="L3917"></a>**L3917** `02:27:42` Heathmore, knight, Viking, samurai, and

<a id="L3918"></a>**L3918** `02:27:45` wuin of a time when there was no

<a id="L3919"></a>**L3919** `02:27:47` animosity between the factions but

<a id="L3920"></a>**L3920** `02:27:49` unity. Many look to Ramiel as an example

<a id="L3921"></a>**L3921** `02:27:51` of one who can put aside their

<a id="L3922"></a>**L3922** `02:27:53` differences to do what is necessary for

<a id="L3923"></a>**L3923** `02:27:55` peace. And this would end Heathmore's

<a id="L3924"></a>**L3924** `02:27:59` golden age.

<a id="L3993"></a>**L3993** `02:30:39` The second of the four relics was one

<a id="L4486"></a>**L4486** `02:49:13` cult of the stake worshiped the four

<a id="L4490"></a>**L4490** `02:49:22` veneration and subjugation to these

<a id="L4491"></a>**L4491** `02:49:24` items would lead to a brighter and

<a id="L4492"></a>**L4492** `02:49:26` better future for Heathmore. But in

<a id="L4493"></a>**L4493** `02:49:29` their attempt to bring converts, they

<a id="L4494"></a>**L4494** `02:49:30` committed horrid atrocities across

<a id="L4495"></a>**L4495** `02:49:32` Ashefeld. Astria responded by forming an

<a id="L4496"></a>**L4496** `02:49:34` inquisition to hunt down and purge this

<a id="L4497"></a>**L4497** `02:49:37` cult. However, the Inquisition proved

<a id="L4499"></a>**L4499** `02:49:41` cult, putting to death and fire any who

<a id="L4500"></a>**L4500** `02:49:44` even came in contact with the cult,

<a id="L4501"></a>**L4501** `02:49:45` including innocent women, children, and

<a id="L4502"></a>**L4502** `02:49:48` elderly. One Inquisitor, Yen Chen,

<a id="L4503"></a>**L4503** `02:49:50` couldn't take the madness any longer.

<a id="L4504"></a>**L4504** `02:49:52` She turned on the Inquisition and waged

<a id="L4505"></a>**L4505** `02:49:54` a onewoman crusade against both the

<a id="L4506"></a>**L4506** `02:49:56` Inquisition and the cult. At last, she

<a id="L4507"></a>**L4507** `02:49:59` managed to steal the four relics and go

<a id="L4508"></a>**L4508** `02:50:01` out to sea with them, hoping that

<a id="L4509"></a>**L4509** `02:50:03` removing them from Heathmore would

<a id="L4510"></a>**L4510** `02:50:04` finally bring peace. For in the end,

<a id="L4513"></a>**L4513** `02:50:17` Yor was for a long time the stem balloon

<a id="L4769"></a>**L4769** `02:59:33` ghost. She trapped the Heathmore people

<a id="L4775"></a>**L4775** `02:59:45` from the clutches of disaster. The

<a id="L4847"></a>**L4847** `03:02:45` All right. Where was I? Ah, yes. For

<a id="L4848"></a>**L4848** `03:02:49` honor fashion. Over the years of Forana,

<a id="L4849"></a>**L4849** `03:02:52` there have been lots of different

<a id="L4850"></a>**L4850** `03:02:53` events. Some are hosted by Foreigners

<a id="L4851"></a>**L4851** `03:02:55` team or Ubisoft themselves, but the

<a id="L4852"></a>**L4852** `03:02:57` great majority are hosted by the

<a id="L4853"></a>**L4853** `03:02:59` community itself. One of the most

<a id="L4854"></a>**L4854** `03:03:01` consistent have been fashion events

<a id="L4855"></a>**L4855** `03:03:03` where members of the community will put

<a id="L4856"></a>**L4856** `03:03:04` together their best outfits in game and

<a id="L4857"></a>**L4857** `03:03:06` then confidently walk out to be judged

<a id="L4858"></a>**L4858** `03:03:08` by the entire community. Even my

<a id="L4859"></a>**L4859** `03:03:10` community, the Tribunal, has hosted a

<a id="L4860"></a>**L4860** `03:03:12` cup in the past, one of which Garrison

<a id="L4861"></a>**L4861** `03:03:14` even won. One of the biggest reasons I

<a id="L4862"></a>**L4862** `03:03:16` believe these events were important is

<a id="L4863"></a>**L4863** `03:03:18` that they weren't limited by skill or

<a id="L4864"></a>**L4864** `03:03:20` following. anyone of any caliber could

<a id="L4865"></a>**L4865** `03:03:22` take part and even have a small chance

<a id="L4866"></a>**L4866** `03:03:24` at winning provided their outfits were

<a id="L4867"></a>**L4867** `03:03:25` unique or entertaining enough. A running

<a id="L4868"></a>**L4868** `03:03:27` joke within my own community was how the

<a id="L4869"></a>**L4869** `03:03:29` sole motivation of playing for honor was

<a id="L4870"></a>**L4870** `03:03:31` that we could keep making more fashion

<a id="L4871"></a>**L4871** `03:03:33` and completing outfits. And even now, it

<a id="L4872"></a>**L4872** `03:03:35` remains an important part of the

<a id="L4873"></a>**L4873** `03:03:36` community with some even making cosplays

<a id="L4874"></a>**L4874** `03:03:38` of their characters or using characters

<a id="L4875"></a>**L4875** `03:03:40` from Funna in other media like Dn D or

<a id="L4876"></a>**L4876** `03:03:42` even in their own writing. With some of

<a id="L4877"></a>**L4877** `03:03:44` the most recent updates in Fora, you can

<a id="L4878"></a>**L4878** `03:03:46` even tweak the individual colors of your

<a id="L4879"></a>**L4879** `03:03:48` outfit using the game's materials to mix

<a id="L4880"></a>**L4880** `03:03:50` and match separate parts of your armor

<a id="L4881"></a>**L4881** `03:03:51` and clothing, or even apply symbols and

<a id="L4882"></a>**L4882** `03:03:53` paint patterns to truly make the style

<a id="L4883"></a>**L4883** `03:03:55` unique. And with the game's emblem

<a id="L4884"></a>**L4884** `03:03:57` system, it allows you to creatively make

<a id="L4885"></a>**L4885** `03:03:59` your own symbols. So, I remember in one

<a id="L4886"></a>**L4886** `03:04:01` of the fashion events I took part in,

<a id="L4887"></a>**L4887** `03:04:03` one of the runner-ups for winning was a

<a id="L4888"></a>**L4888** `03:04:05` guy that was just Pepsi man. He had a

<a id="L4889"></a>**L4889** `03:04:07` massive Pepsi logo struck to his chest

<a id="L4890"></a>**L4890** `03:04:09` and that managed to with the community's

<a id="L4891"></a>**L4891** `03:04:11` boosting basically saw all the way up

<a id="L4892"></a>**L4892** `03:04:14` the rankings on that fashion event. All

<a id="L4893"></a>**L4893** `03:04:16` in all, I feel like Foreigners fashion

<a id="L4894"></a>**L4894** `03:04:18` is an integral part of the game and even

<a id="L4895"></a>**L4895** `03:04:21` through its 9 plus years, it's still

<a id="L4896"></a>**L4896** `03:04:24` such an important part to the community

<a id="L4897"></a>**L4897** `03:04:26` and the game and the lead focus of why

<a id="L4898"></a>**L4898** `03:04:28` many people continue to play it.

<a id="L4899"></a>**L4899** `03:04:32` Astria already had a strong foothold in

<a id="L4900"></a>**L4900** `03:04:34` Valenheim, but her control wasn't

<a id="L4901"></a>**L4901** `03:04:36` complete. The village of Maldder still

<a id="L4902"></a>**L4902** `03:04:39` remained, ruled by a wise Yarl Scard and

<a id="L4903"></a>**L4903** `03:04:41` his sworn brother Maddox. Scarred had

<a id="L4904"></a>**L4904** `03:04:44` sworn his oath to Chimera and would

<a id="L4905"></a>**L4905** `03:04:45` never surrender to Horcos, which was

<a id="L4906"></a>**L4906** `03:04:47` unfortunate as Horcos had now surrounded

<a id="L4907"></a>**L4907** `03:04:50` Maldder and were preparing to invade it.

<a id="L4908"></a>**L4908** `03:04:52` They offered a choice to Yarl Scard.

<a id="L4909"></a>**L4909** `03:04:55` Surrender and join Horcos or refuse and

<a id="L4910"></a>**L4910** `03:04:57` be slaughtered. Scard would not

<a id="L4911"></a>**L4911** `03:04:59` surrender, for oaths are not only meant

<a id="L4912"></a>**L4912** `03:05:01` to be upheld when convenient. If he must

<a id="L4913"></a>**L4913** `03:05:04` die for his oath and his honor, then so

<a id="L4914"></a>**L4914** `03:05:06` be it. But his sworn brother, Maddox,

<a id="L4915"></a>**L4915** `03:05:09` had no such intention. He was a survivor

<a id="L4916"></a>**L4916** `03:05:12` at any cost. When it was clear his sworn

<a id="L4917"></a>**L4917** `03:05:14` brother would never relent, he staged a

<a id="L4918"></a>**L4918** `03:05:16` coup. He killed his sworn brother,

<a id="L4919"></a>**L4919** `03:05:18` Scard, and led an internal rebellion,

<a id="L4920"></a>**L4920** `03:05:20` slaughtering all the Vikings loyal to

<a id="L4921"></a>**L4921** `03:05:22` the YL and taking control of the village

<a id="L4922"></a>**L4922** `03:05:24` himself, swearing feelalty to Horos.

<a id="L4923"></a>**L4923** `03:05:26` However, he missed one individual,

<a id="L4924"></a>**L4924** `03:05:29` Katla, Scarard's daughter, who fled from

<a id="L4925"></a>**L4925** `03:05:31` Maldder just in time. She found Holden

<a id="L4926"></a>**L4926** `03:05:34` Cross, who instructed her to seek out

<a id="L4927"></a>**L4927** `03:05:36` the mercenary man known as the Vangian

<a id="L4928"></a>**L4928** `03:05:38` Guard. These mighty mercenary warriors

<a id="L4929"></a>**L4929** `03:05:41` were renowned for their tenacity and

<a id="L4930"></a>**L4930** `03:05:43` their loyalty. They would never break

<a id="L4931"></a>**L4931** `03:05:45` the os. And with Cotlin now with them,

<a id="L4932"></a>**L4932** `03:05:47` they launched an attack on Maldder to

<a id="L4933"></a>**L4933** `03:05:49` punish Maddox for his crimes. And though

<a id="L4934"></a>**L4934** `03:05:51` Maddox attempted to buy off the guard,

<a id="L4935"></a>**L4935** `03:05:53` they could not be purchased. and Maddox

<a id="L4936"></a>**L4936** `03:05:56` was defeated with Moulder being returned

<a id="L4937"></a>**L4937** `03:05:58` to Cotless control. She had finally

<a id="L4938"></a>**L4938** `03:06:00` brought an end to her uncle's treason.

<a id="L4984"></a>**L4984** `03:09:12` You lost your

<a id="L4985"></a>**L4985** `03:09:45` When the cataclysm struck Ashefeld over

<a id="L4986"></a>**L4986** `03:09:47` a thousand years ago, legends tell of a

<a id="L4987"></a>**L4987** `03:09:50` single warden who had gone on to be

<a id="L4988"></a>**L4988** `03:09:52` called the unsung warden. Her name lost

<a id="L4989"></a>**L4989** `03:09:54` to time. She was known for wielding the

<a id="L4990"></a>**L4990** `03:09:56` legendary sword of Ashefeld. It was said

<a id="L4991"></a>**L4991** `03:09:59` that when the orders and knights of

<a id="L4992"></a>**L4992** `03:10:00` Ashefeld were divided, the one who held

<a id="L4993"></a>**L4993** `03:10:02` this sword would unite them again. When

<a id="L4994"></a>**L4994** `03:10:04` the Vikings and samurai invaded Ashefeld

<a id="L4995"></a>**L4995** `03:10:06` to plunder its resources, it was the

<a id="L4996"></a>**L4996** `03:10:08` unsung knight who emerged and with the

<a id="L4997"></a>**L4997** `03:10:11` United legions of Ashefeld behind her,

<a id="L4998"></a>**L4998** `03:10:13` pushed back the invaders, saving not

<a id="L4999"></a>**L4999** `03:10:15` only Ashefeld, but the ways of the

<a id="L5000"></a>**L5000** `03:10:17` knights. Though lost now, all remember

<a id="L5001"></a>**L5001** `03:10:20` the meaning of the sword of Ashefeld.

<a id="L5002"></a>**L5002** `03:10:27` Year 8 started with a banger because the

<a id="L5026"></a>**L5026** `03:11:21` collaboration decision with Etsio being

<a id="L5028"></a>**L5028** `03:11:26` even changing the voice lines for him.

<a id="L5029"></a>**L5029** `03:11:28` Also, magic got buffed and nerfed at the

<a id="L5030"></a>**L5030** `03:11:30` same time. Now, he can do a zone in the

<a id="L5031"></a>**L5031** `03:11:32` middle of his chains to switch between

<a id="L5032"></a>**L5032** `03:11:34` his guards, but his neutral bash got

<a id="L5033"></a>**L5033** `03:11:36` removed same way as for Warlord a few

<a id="L5034"></a>**L5034** `03:11:38` seasons before, and his lights no longer

<a id="L5035"></a>**L5035** `03:11:40` have hyper armor, kicking match out from

<a id="L5036"></a>**L5036** `03:11:42` the top three, and now Shinobi gets back

<a id="L5037"></a>**L5037** `03:11:44` into the ranks. And on April Fools, they

<a id="L5038"></a>**L5038** `03:11:47` came up with something interesting this

<a id="L5039"></a>**L5039** `03:11:48` year. All heroes had their weapons

<a id="L5040"></a>**L5040** `03:11:50` switched around with other heroes. The

<a id="L5041"></a>**L5041** `03:11:52` switch rule, they called it. I have to

<a id="L5042"></a>**L5042** `03:11:54` give Ubisoft credit where credit is due.

<a id="L5043"></a>**L5043** `03:11:57` Their April Fool events never

<a id="L5044"></a>**L5044** `03:11:58` disappoint.

<a id="L5045"></a>**L5045** `03:12:10` When I started the dual league, there

<a id="L5046"></a>**L5046** `03:12:12` were no duel tournaments, bro. I was the

<a id="L5047"></a>**L5047** `03:12:13` only one. And I was viciously mocked by

<a id="L5048"></a>**L5048** `03:12:16` the competitive community at this point

<a id="L5049"></a>**L5049** `03:12:18` because you know they were segregated

<a id="L5050"></a>**L5050** `03:12:19` but I didn't care about like I knew that

<a id="L5051"></a>**L5051** `03:12:21` duels were was like the future of the

<a id="L5052"></a>**L5052** `03:12:23` game. I wanted to make it like ranked

<a id="L5053"></a>**L5053** `03:12:25` but good, right? So low Q times you just

<a id="L5054"></a>**L5054** `03:12:28` show up, you drop your gamertag in the

<a id="L5055"></a>**L5055** `03:12:29` chat and I find you games and you would

<a id="L5056"></a>**L5056** `03:12:31` go and play against a wide variety of

<a id="L5057"></a>**L5057** `03:12:33` players. Most of them very very very

<a id="L5058"></a>**L5058** `03:12:35` good. Some of them the best in the

<a id="L5059"></a>**L5059** `03:12:37` world, some of them garbage, you know,

<a id="L5060"></a>**L5060** `03:12:39` but we allowed anybody to play and it

<a id="L5061"></a>**L5061** `03:12:41` was um first of five best two to threes.

<a id="L5062"></a>**L5062** `03:12:43` you would potentially have to play, you

<a id="L5063"></a>**L5063** `03:12:45` know, the number seven player, the

<a id="L5064"></a>**L5064** `03:12:47` number 15 player, the number one player,

<a id="L5065"></a>**L5065** `03:12:48` and then the number five player all in a

<a id="L5066"></a>**L5066** `03:12:50` row. And this is where Braden really got

<a id="L5067"></a>**L5067** `03:12:52` the reputation as clearly the best

<a id="L5068"></a>**L5068** `03:12:53` duelist on Xbox. He went 26-1 in sets,

<a id="L5069"></a>**L5069** `03:12:57` 53 and three in games, which is a level

<a id="L5070"></a>**L5070** `03:13:00` of dominance never before seen in the

<a id="L5071"></a>**L5071** `03:13:02` FHL. Really, you played a lot. Garand

<a id="L5072"></a>**L5072** `03:13:05` still has the record for the longest win

<a id="L5073"></a>**L5073** `03:13:07` streak ever in the FHL history, which is

<a id="L5074"></a>**L5074** `03:13:09` 26. Still to this day, stands. Loved

<a id="L5075"></a>**L5075** `03:13:12` you. I always appreciated you.

<a id="L5076"></a>**L5076** `03:13:13` Immortalum almost lost to Kenobi. Kenobi

<a id="L5077"></a>**L5077** `03:13:16` was a perennial punching bag right in

<a id="L5078"></a>**L5078** `03:13:19` the FHDL. Guys that play a lot and they

<a id="L5079"></a>**L5079** `03:13:21` don't win a lot. We I love those guys

<a id="L5080"></a>**L5080** `03:13:23` though. You're talking about an extreme

<a id="L5081"></a>**L5081** `03:13:24` level of mental fortitude to do that,

<a id="L5082"></a>**L5082** `03:13:26` you know, day in day out. Keep losing.

<a id="L5083"></a>**L5083** `03:13:28` So all time he's 18 and 66 and

<a id="L5084"></a>**L5084** `03:13:31` Immortalum is considered like one of the

<a id="L5086"></a>**L5086** `03:13:35` Kenobi almost won. Literally cursing out

<a id="L5087"></a>**L5087** `03:13:37` Immortal for light spamming him. And

<a id="L5088"></a>**L5088** `03:13:39` unironically, Immortal had to like spam

<a id="L5089"></a>**L5089** `03:13:42` him to win cuz uh Kenobi was on old gen.

<a id="L5090"></a>**L5090** `03:13:44` I remember that one. That was a really

<a id="L5091"></a>**L5091** `03:13:45` good one. I have to talk about the open.

<a id="L5092"></a>**L5092** `03:13:47` So before I went on my long break, I'm

<a id="L5093"></a>**L5093** `03:13:49` going to get the best 16 players in the

<a id="L5094"></a>**L5094** `03:13:51` world and we're going to do a double

<a id="L5095"></a>**L5095** `03:13:53` elimination tournament. I got Braden, I

<a id="L5096"></a>**L5096** `03:13:55` got Bean, and that was the first time

<a id="L5097"></a>**L5097** `03:13:57` they had ever been in a tournament

<a id="L5098"></a>**L5098** `03:13:58` together. There was these really big

<a id="L5099"></a>**L5099** `03:13:59` question. Is Braen the best player read

<a id="L5100"></a>**L5100** `03:14:01` player on Xbox from the United States?

<a id="L5101"></a>**L5101** `03:14:04` or is it being this reaction freak from

<a id="L5102"></a>**L5102** `03:14:06` England that consistently won in

<a id="L5103"></a>**L5103** `03:14:09` tournaments? Also had guys like Totine,

<a id="L5104"></a>**L5104** `03:14:11` Blitz, Ewab, Tuba, guys that were proven

<a id="L5105"></a>**L5105** `03:14:14` at the highest level. Like this

<a id="L5106"></a>**L5106** `03:14:15` tournament was insane. I think one of

<a id="L5107"></a>**L5107** `03:14:17` the really big ones was SG. Absolutely

<a id="L5108"></a>**L5108** `03:14:19` throttling Totine in the first round.

<a id="L5109"></a>**L5109** `03:14:21` Huge upset that nobody saw. But the next

<a id="L5110"></a>**L5110** `03:14:24` game would be even bigger. It was Blitz

<a id="L5111"></a>**L5111** `03:14:27` versus SG. That game, you can watch it

<a id="L5112"></a>**L5112** `03:14:29` on my channel, is one of the greatest

<a id="L5113"></a>**L5113** `03:14:31` for Honor games I've ever witnessed.

<a id="L5114"></a>**L5114** `03:14:34` Bean would eventually win. Uh he won the

<a id="L5115"></a>**L5115** `03:14:36` first ones against Braden. Braden won

<a id="L5116"></a>**L5116** `03:14:38` the second set and then Bean won the

<a id="L5117"></a>**L5117** `03:14:40` third. And very unfortunately, it made

<a id="L5118"></a>**L5118** `03:14:42` me realize that the game was still

<a id="L5119"></a>**L5119** `03:14:44` broken and favored towards reactors. You

<a id="L5120"></a>**L5120** `03:14:47` know, it just felt like Braden kind of

<a id="L5121"></a>**L5121** `03:14:48` got cheesed and Bean could react to

<a id="L5122"></a>**L5122** `03:14:51` basically everything. And my goal was to

<a id="L5123"></a>**L5123** `03:14:54` develop the game in 1v ones. And I

<a id="L5124"></a>**L5124** `03:14:57` sacrificed a lot of time to do this. As

<a id="L5125"></a>**L5125** `03:14:59` you know, Garan, you you were very aware

<a id="L5126"></a>**L5126** `03:15:01` of how much time I would sacrifice for

<a id="L5127"></a>**L5127** `03:15:02` this. And I was very successful at it,

<a id="L5128"></a>**L5128** `03:15:04` you know, even to my own detriment as a

<a id="L5129"></a>**L5129** `03:15:06` player. Very much enjoy the fact that

<a id="L5130"></a>**L5130** `03:15:08` there's all of these players now that

<a id="L5131"></a>**L5131** `03:15:10` are so much better than me. Didn't used

<a id="L5132"></a>**L5132** `03:15:12` to be the case. And it's been really

<a id="L5133"></a>**L5133** `03:15:14` cool to see the competitive side of

<a id="L5134"></a>**L5134** `03:15:16` honor really grow, man. Like, it gives

<a id="L5136"></a>**L5136** `03:15:22` There once was a samurai swordsmith

<a id="L5137"></a>**L5137** `03:15:24` named Muramasa who crafted a sword

<a id="L5138"></a>**L5138** `03:15:26` filled with hate, malice, and

<a id="L5139"></a>**L5139** `03:15:28` bitterness. This sword, the Muramasa

<a id="L5140"></a>**L5140** `03:15:30` blade, was said to give its user the

<a id="L5141"></a>**L5141** `03:15:32` strength of a 100 men. But in return, it

<a id="L5142"></a>**L5142** `03:15:34` filled the user with unquenchable rage,

<a id="L5143"></a>**L5143** `03:15:36` and fury. Perhaps it was faked then that

<a id="L5144"></a>**L5144** `03:15:38` the sword was found by Master Katashi, a

<a id="L5145"></a>**L5145** `03:15:41` famed Orochi who had mastered his mind

<a id="L5146"></a>**L5146** `03:15:42` and body through meditation in the study

<a id="L5147"></a>**L5147** `03:15:44` of Zen. He now wields the sword and

<a id="L5148"></a>**L5148** `03:15:47` controls both himself and the blade in

<a id="L5149"></a>**L5149** `03:15:49` order to protect a sacred bridge into

<a id="L5150"></a>**L5150** `03:15:51` the mire. But the power of Katashi had

<a id="L5151"></a>**L5151** `03:15:53` spread far and reached the ears of the

<a id="L5152"></a>**L5152** `03:15:55` deadly Sohi monk, Oniwaka. Oniwaka was a

<a id="L5153"></a>**L5153** `03:15:58` master of seven weapons and sought to

<a id="L5154"></a>**L5154** `03:16:00` claim the Moramasa sword for himself.

<a id="L5155"></a>**L5155** `03:16:02` And when he duled Katashi, the two

<a id="L5156"></a>**L5156** `03:16:04` fought to a standstill. Neither were

<a id="L5157"></a>**L5157** `03:16:06` able to overcome the other. And it was

<a id="L5158"></a>**L5158** `03:16:08` then that Oniwaka truly felt humbled and

<a id="L5159"></a>**L5159** `03:16:11` chose to learn from Katashi. Together,

<a id="L5160"></a>**L5160** `03:16:13` they rebuilt the concept of the Sohi and

<a id="L5161"></a>**L5161** `03:16:15` began teaching the way of seven weapons

<a id="L5162"></a>**L5162** `03:16:17` to others so that more Sohi could rise

<a id="L5163"></a>**L5163** `03:16:20` and defend the Meer alongside the master

<a id="L5164"></a>**L5164** `03:16:22` of the Muramasa blade.

<a id="L5165"></a>**L5165** `03:17:02` SOA is a heavy hero with 140 HP that has

<a id="L5166"></a>**L5166** `03:17:07` a very different play style to everyone

<a id="L5167"></a>**L5167** `03:17:09` else in the game. So deals little to no

<a id="L5168"></a>**L5168** `03:17:12` damage with his noodle arms and has six

<a id="L5169"></a>**L5169** `03:17:14` weapons for whatever reason. His overall

<a id="L5170"></a>**L5170** `03:17:17` lower damage and his insane health pool

<a id="L5171"></a>**L5171** `03:17:21` lead to him playing the game a whole lot

<a id="L5172"></a>**L5172** `03:17:24` different than you would imagine. Sua

<a id="L5173"></a>**L5173** `03:17:26` utilizes his early hyper armor in order

<a id="L5174"></a>**L5174** `03:17:28` to trade with opponents and gain

<a id="L5175"></a>**L5175** `03:17:31` momentum that way. I always like to

<a id="L5176"></a>**L5176** `03:17:33` imagine it like his health pool is his

<a id="L5177"></a>**L5177** `03:17:36` main resource. The lower his health is,

<a id="L5178"></a>**L5178** `03:17:38` the weaker he becomes. He can cheat his

<a id="L5179"></a>**L5179** `03:17:41` way out of the neutral game with his son

<a id="L5180"></a>**L5180** `03:17:43` attack and regain momentum in an

<a id="L5181"></a>**L5181** `03:17:45` instant. So soul make him into the hero

<a id="L5182"></a>**L5182** `03:17:48` he is. He has six unique souls, all of

<a id="L5183"></a>**L5183** `03:17:51` them being gained by landing a combo

<a id="L5184"></a>**L5184** `03:17:54` heavy finisher, either with his lights

<a id="L5185"></a>**L5185** `03:17:56` or his heavies. He can utilize his front

<a id="L5186"></a>**L5186** `03:17:59` step bash in order to start some

<a id="L5187"></a>**L5187** `03:18:01` momentum. But everything he does is

<a id="L5188"></a>**L5188** `03:18:03` based around one simple fact. He has a

<a id="L5189"></a>**L5189** `03:18:06` built-in Duke in his base kit. If he

<a id="L5190"></a>**L5190** `03:18:09` acquires each and every one of his

<a id="L5191"></a>**L5191** `03:18:11` souls, he can have a 95 damage swing.

<a id="L5192"></a>**L5192** `03:18:13` This leads to all of his attacks apart

<a id="L5193"></a>**L5193** `03:18:15` from neutral attacks dealing little to

<a id="L5194"></a>**L5194** `03:18:17` no damage. So in duels and so in

<a id="L5195"></a>**L5195** `03:18:20` Dominion are completely different

<a id="L5196"></a>**L5196** `03:18:22` dimensions. He can't really output the

<a id="L5197"></a>**L5197** `03:18:26` amount of pressure you need out of a

<a id="L5198"></a>**L5198** `03:18:28` hero in 1 v ones. He isn't nearly fast

<a id="L5199"></a>**L5199** `03:18:31` enough to not be reactable. On the other

<a id="L5200"></a>**L5200** `03:18:33` hand, his ability to gain three out of

<a id="L5201"></a>**L5201** `03:18:36` the six souls by just landing one guard

<a id="L5202"></a>**L5202** `03:18:38` break makes it so that mistakes against

<a id="L5203"></a>**L5203** `03:18:40` him basically already leave you halfway

<a id="L5204"></a>**L5204** `03:18:42` out of the game. If he gets all six of

<a id="L5205"></a>**L5205** `03:18:44` them, you're out. You have to understand

<a id="L5206"></a>**L5206** `03:18:47` SOA is held back by two things. Low

<a id="L5207"></a>**L5207** `03:18:50` damage and low stamina. Dominion gives

<a id="L5208"></a>**L5208** `03:18:53` Suhai the answer to both of his

<a id="L5209"></a>**L5209** `03:18:55` weaknesses in the form of incillary

<a id="L5210"></a>**L5210** `03:18:57` might and with his perk endurance. His

<a id="L5211"></a>**L5211** `03:19:01` first feat, Eye of the Demon, is

<a id="L5212"></a>**L5212** `03:19:03` possibly the strongest first feat in the

<a id="L5213"></a>**L5213** `03:19:05` game. By the press of a button, you can

<a id="L5214"></a>**L5214** `03:19:07` debuff an opponent from attacks of your

<a id="L5215"></a>**L5215** `03:19:09` entire team. His second feat, Soul

<a id="L5216"></a>**L5216** `03:19:12` Sufer, is the only reason he's viable in

<a id="L5217"></a>**L5217** `03:19:15` Dominion. Every one of you out there who

<a id="L5218"></a>**L5218** `03:19:18` does not play Soul Sufer, you listen to

<a id="L5219"></a>**L5219** `03:19:20` me right now, okay? I will find you and

<a id="L5220"></a>**L5220** `03:19:22` I will hack into your computer and

<a id="L5221"></a>**L5221** `03:19:25` re-equip Souls Sufer because how dare

<a id="L5222"></a>**L5222** `03:19:27` you use smoke bomb on him. With a very

<a id="L5223"></a>**L5223** `03:19:30` short activation time, Sufa restores the

<a id="L5224"></a>**L5224** `03:19:33` entirety of SOA's health bar in an

<a id="L5225"></a>**L5225** `03:19:36` instant. So can pick between many

<a id="L5226"></a>**L5226** `03:19:38` different designer brands to maximize

<a id="L5227"></a>**L5227** `03:19:40` his grip. All of his different armor

<a id="L5228"></a>**L5228** `03:19:42` variations make him look completely

<a id="L5229"></a>**L5229** `03:19:44` different with plated armor or wooden

<a id="L5230"></a>**L5230** `03:19:46` armor across the board. One thing that

<a id="L5231"></a>**L5231** `03:19:48` sadly stays the same, but would be too

<a id="L5232"></a>**L5232** `03:19:51` much effort, is changing how his weapons

<a id="L5233"></a>**L5233** `03:19:53` look. His six weapons on his back look.

<a id="L5234"></a>**L5234** `03:19:56` When it comes to face masks, he also has

<a id="L5235"></a>**L5235** `03:19:58` some pretty cool options. Looking like a

<a id="L5236"></a>**L5236** `03:20:00` grumpy old demon, looking like a grumpy

<a id="L5237"></a>**L5237** `03:20:03` old man, and looking like a grumpy old

<a id="L5238"></a>**L5238** `03:20:05` man with a different beard. But I just

<a id="L5239"></a>**L5239** `03:20:08` have to pick his normal, his base kit

<a id="L5240"></a>**L5240** `03:20:10` masks since it has become so iconic.

<a id="L5241"></a>**L5241** `03:20:15` One thing that people kept complaining

<a id="L5242"></a>**L5242** `03:20:17` about for years was no new maps in like

<a id="L5243"></a>**L5243** `03:20:19` half a decade. But Ubisoft didn't have a

<a id="L5244"></a>**L5244** `03:20:21` budget to make some, so they decided to

<a id="L5245"></a>**L5245** `03:20:23` convert elimination only maps into

<a id="L5246"></a>**L5246** `03:20:25` Dominion, starting with Cathedral the

<a id="L5247"></a>**L5247** `03:20:27` season before and now Viking Village 2.

<a id="L5248"></a>**L5248** `03:20:30` Something to notice how Ubisoft switched

<a id="L5249"></a>**L5249** `03:20:32` the content release around over the

<a id="L5250"></a>**L5250** `03:20:33` later years when the game had a larger

<a id="L5251"></a>**L5251** `03:20:35` budget. Every hero became unique emotes

<a id="L5252"></a>**L5252** `03:20:37` and executions almost every season. Then

<a id="L5253"></a>**L5253** `03:20:40` the budget got cut and they started to

<a id="L5254"></a>**L5254** `03:20:42` bring out universal rewards and

<a id="L5255"></a>**L5255** `03:20:43` executions that the community didn't

<a id="L5256"></a>**L5256** `03:20:45` like. So after enough North Backlash,

<a id="L5257"></a>**L5257** `03:20:47` they came up with a pretty good idea.

<a id="L5258"></a>**L5258** `03:20:48` Hero fests. Each season we got like two

<a id="L5259"></a>**L5259** `03:20:51` hero fests where individual heroes got

<a id="L5260"></a>**L5260** `03:20:53` unique executions and such instead of

<a id="L5261"></a>**L5261** `03:20:55` universal stuff for everyone. This way

<a id="L5262"></a>**L5262** `03:20:57` heroes are still able to express

<a id="L5263"></a>**L5263** `03:20:59` themselves uniquely while also staying

<a id="L5264"></a>**L5264** `03:21:01` on budget for the now smaller for honor

<a id="L5265"></a>**L5265** `03:21:03` staff.

<a id="L5266"></a>**L5266** `03:21:05` Perhaps no Yarl in all of Valenheim was

<a id="L5267"></a>**L5267** `03:21:08` as celebrated and beloved as Yarl

<a id="L5268"></a>**L5268** `03:21:10` Goodmunder the Great, a man who is said

<a id="L5269"></a>**L5269** `03:21:12` to have wielded the shield and sword of

<a id="L5270"></a>**L5270** `03:21:14` Odin himself. After his death during the

<a id="L5271"></a>**L5271** `03:21:16` siege of Zvenguard, the warriors of

<a id="L5272"></a>**L5272** `03:21:18` Valkconheim came together to honor his

<a id="L5273"></a>**L5273** `03:21:20` passing in the traditional way of the

<a id="L5274"></a>**L5274** `03:21:22` Vikings. In keeping with the tradition,

<a id="L5275"></a>**L5275** `03:21:23` it was required the shield and sword be

<a id="L5276"></a>**L5276** `03:21:25` broken so no other may use them. But

<a id="L5277"></a>**L5277** `03:21:27` they would not break. No matter what

<a id="L5278"></a>**L5278** `03:21:30` method was used, the shield did not

<a id="L5279"></a>**L5279** `03:21:31` shatter. And it was then that good's

<a id="L5280"></a>**L5280** `03:21:34` legend was solidified. Just like the

<a id="L5281"></a>**L5281** `03:21:36` great man who wielded it, nothing could

<a id="L5282"></a>**L5282** `03:21:38` break the shield of Svenguard.

<a id="L5283"></a>**L5283** `03:21:44` So, in year 8 season 3, Lawbringer got

<a id="L5284"></a>**L5284** `03:21:48` the update to where he can bash after

<a id="L5285"></a>**L5285** `03:21:50` any light attack, which was an insane

<a id="L5286"></a>**L5286** `03:21:52` change because not only does that give

<a id="L5287"></a>**L5287** `03:21:55` him more options after his light opener

<a id="L5288"></a>**L5288** `03:21:57` cuz beforehand he can only, you know,

<a id="L5289"></a>**L5289** `03:21:59` light into heavy, having the bash for

<a id="L5290"></a>**L5290** `03:22:00` the additional pressure essentially gave

<a id="L5291"></a>**L5291** `03:22:02` him a mixup at any point that was not

<a id="L5292"></a>**L5292** `03:22:05` just block. It puts him directly into

<a id="L5293"></a>**L5293** `03:22:08` his finisher after he he hits it and

<a id="L5294"></a>**L5294** `03:22:10` gets his confirmed light. Not only that,

<a id="L5295"></a>**L5295** `03:22:12` they gave him somewhat like a centurion

<a id="L5296"></a>**L5296** `03:22:14` punch combo where he could just keep

<a id="L5297"></a>**L5297** `03:22:16` going into it over and over. Bash light,

<a id="L5298"></a>**L5298** `03:22:18` bash light. Man, it makes lobing one of

<a id="L5299"></a>**L5299** `03:22:20` the scariest picks out there. Like I've

<a id="L5300"></a>**L5300** `03:22:22` watched some competitive streams and

<a id="L5301"></a>**L5301** `03:22:24` people are expecting people to choose

<a id="L5302"></a>**L5302** `03:22:26` Lawbringer in ranked because he is just

<a id="L5303"></a>**L5303** `03:22:28` a scary pick. And his mixup, it's not

<a id="L5304"></a>**L5304** `03:22:30` one of those where you can like react to

<a id="L5305"></a>**L5305** `03:22:33` parry flash and parry it, you know, and

<a id="L5306"></a>**L5306** `03:22:35` you got to you got to make the right

<a id="L5307"></a>**L5307** `03:22:37` read if they're going to do the bash or

<a id="L5308"></a>**L5308** `03:22:39` the unblockable. It was just significant

<a id="L5309"></a>**L5309** `03:22:41` for him. One more thing I want to

<a id="L5310"></a>**L5310** `03:22:42` mention. Long arm is no longer a bad

<a id="L5311"></a>**L5311** `03:22:45` move for duels, guys. It's no longer a

<a id="L5312"></a>**L5312** `03:22:47` bad move. It's now safe and you get 29

<a id="L5313"></a>**L5313** `03:22:49` damage if you hit it. Okay? So, stop

<a id="L5314"></a>**L5314** `03:22:52` saying long arm is bad. It's not bad.

<a id="L5315"></a>**L5315** `03:22:54` The recovery is stinky. But that's it.

<a id="L5316"></a>**L5316** `03:22:57` The damage is good and it's safe and you

<a id="L5317"></a>**L5317** `03:22:59` can go into more mixups after. That's

<a id="L5318"></a>**L5318** `03:23:00` all I got to say for all the haters out

<a id="L5319"></a>**L5319** `03:23:02` there.

<a id="L5320"></a>**L5320** `03:23:06` Besides Linger's buff, we also saw a big

<a id="L5321"></a>**L5321** `03:23:08` ocelot nerf this season. They removed

<a id="L5322"></a>**L5322** `03:23:11` his ability to throw a light attack

<a id="L5323"></a>**L5323** `03:23:13` after whiffing his bash. While he was

<a id="L5324"></a>**L5324** `03:23:15` never a top three character in any game

<a id="L5325"></a>**L5325** `03:23:17` mode, he was a strong character,

<a id="L5326"></a>**L5326** `03:23:19` especially in the minion. But with this

<a id="L5327"></a>**L5327** `03:23:20` change, he became rather irrelevant to

<a id="L5328"></a>**L5328** `03:23:23` the meta. Lastly, we did not get a

<a id="L5329"></a>**L5329** `03:23:25` Halloween event this season, but instead

<a id="L5330"></a>**L5330** `03:23:27` another crossover event, this time with

<a id="L5331"></a>**L5331** `03:23:30` Destiny.

<a id="L5332"></a>**L5332** `03:23:31` >> So, Light of the Guardians. Now, a lot

<a id="L5333"></a>**L5333** `03:23:33` of people have a lot of opinions on

<a id="L5334"></a>**L5334** `03:23:35` this. A lot of people loved it. Me

<a id="L5335"></a>**L5335** `03:23:37` personally, I hated it. I was expecting

<a id="L5336"></a>**L5336** `03:23:41` a Halloween event. They cut corners and

<a id="L5337"></a>**L5337** `03:23:43` released a Destiny crossover event

<a id="L5338"></a>**L5338** `03:23:45` instead. It was just another twist and

<a id="L5339"></a>**L5339** `03:23:48` spin on a minion focused Dominion. You

<a id="L5340"></a>**L5340** `03:23:53` like obtained a certain power and

<a id="L5341"></a>**L5341** `03:23:55` whatnot after a certain point and you

<a id="L5342"></a>**L5342** `03:23:57` could use it on A and C. It was

<a id="L5343"></a>**L5343** `03:23:59` objective focused with these abilities.

<a id="L5344"></a>**L5344** `03:24:02` It was weird. Everything was purple,

<a id="L5345"></a>**L5345** `03:24:04` Destiny themed. The customization for

<a id="L5346"></a>**L5346** `03:24:06` Centurion and Lawbringer was cool.

<a id="L5347"></a>**L5347** `03:24:08` However, having no boss to fight after

<a id="L5348"></a>**L5348** `03:24:12` having 3 years straight of somewhat

<a id="L5349"></a>**L5349** `03:24:14` having cool bosses. That's all I got to

<a id="L5350"></a>**L5350** `03:24:17` say for that one.

<a id="L5351"></a>**L5351** `03:24:20` Astria has almost conquered Heathmore.

<a id="L5352"></a>**L5352** `03:24:23` One push, one last great victory was all

<a id="L5353"></a>**L5353** `03:24:26` she needed. But she knew it was a

<a id="L5354"></a>**L5354** `03:24:28` gamble. Though she had not shown it, the

<a id="L5355"></a>**L5355** `03:24:30` setbacks she'd suffered thanks to the

<a id="L5356"></a>**L5356** `03:24:32` Ocelot, the Vingians, and the Afira were

<a id="L5357"></a>**L5357** `03:24:34` beginning to take their toll. She needed

<a id="L5358"></a>**L5358** `03:24:36` a Hail Mary, some kind of advantage. And

<a id="L5359"></a>**L5359** `03:24:40` she got it when she received a message

<a id="L5360"></a>**L5360** `03:24:42` from General J, an infamous general in

<a id="L5361"></a>**L5361** `03:24:44` the forces of the Wuin. He proposed an

<a id="L5362"></a>**L5362** `03:24:47` alliance. He would offer his Woolen

<a id="L5363"></a>**L5363** `03:24:49` forces to Astria. And in exchange, when

<a id="L5364"></a>**L5364** `03:24:51` she conquered Heathmore, she would leave

<a id="L5365"></a>**L5365** `03:24:53` all Wuen in control under his command,

<a id="L5366"></a>**L5366** `03:24:56` naming him the emperor of the Wuen. To

<a id="L5367"></a>**L5367** `03:24:59` mark this occasion, he would present her

<a id="L5368"></a>**L5368** `03:25:01` with his prize sword, the Starfall

<a id="L5369"></a>**L5369** `03:25:03` sword. Astra was excited, but wary, for

<a id="L5370"></a>**L5370** `03:25:05` J was an opportunistic snake, ambitious

<a id="L5371"></a>**L5371** `03:25:08` and cunning. She knew he couldn't be

<a id="L5372"></a>**L5372** `03:25:10` trusted, but at the same time, she could

<a id="L5373"></a>**L5373** `03:25:12` not overlook this opportunity. She

<a id="L5374"></a>**L5374** `03:25:14` rallied the bulk of her Horos army to

<a id="L5375"></a>**L5375** `03:25:16` meet Jer at the harbor of Itravatnan.

<a id="L5376"></a>**L5376** `03:25:18` There, J and Astra would meet and Joe

<a id="L5377"></a>**L5377** `03:25:21` would reveal his true goals. He had made

<a id="L5378"></a>**L5378** `03:25:23` a prior alliance with another force, the

<a id="L5379"></a>**L5379** `03:25:26` Hatun. These Mongolian warrior women had

<a id="L5380"></a>**L5380** `03:25:29` an ancient score to settle with

<a id="L5381"></a>**L5381** `03:25:30` Heathmore. And Gulgene, their leader,

<a id="L5382"></a>**L5382** `03:25:32` had agreed to fight with J and name him

<a id="L5383"></a>**L5383** `03:25:34` Emperor of the Wuin if he led Astria

<a id="L5384"></a>**L5384** `03:25:37` into this trap. For the Hatun do not

<a id="L5385"></a>**L5385** `03:25:40` seek war, but peace. Peace by the sword.

<a id="L5386"></a>**L5386** `03:25:44` Astra was unprepared, unaware, and at

<a id="L5387"></a>**L5387** `03:25:46` last the shewolf was defeated by Gulene.

<a id="L5388"></a>**L5388** `03:25:50` As Jur looked on at the demise of Astra,

<a id="L5389"></a>**L5389** `03:25:52` he knew, as they all did, that this was

<a id="L5390"></a>**L5390** `03:25:54` the beginning of a new era for

<a id="L5391"></a>**L5391** `03:25:56` Heathmore. One for good or ill, none can

<a id="L5392"></a>**L5392** `03:25:59` say. But when it exchanges hand, only

<a id="L5393"></a>**L5393** `03:26:02` downfall can come from the serpent

<a id="L5394"></a>**L5394** `03:26:05` sword.

<a id="L5395"></a>**L5395** `03:26:08` Katun puts a twist on wellestablished

<a id="L5396"></a>**L5396** `03:26:10` mechanics that we had in the game since

<a id="L5397"></a>**L5397** `03:26:12` the beginning. Her main way to start a

<a id="L5398"></a>**L5398** `03:26:14` fight are her soft fence, creating a

<a id="L5399"></a>**L5399** `03:26:16` unconventional way of attacking. Her

<a id="L5400"></a>**L5400** `03:26:18` running stance can both be used as a

<a id="L5401"></a>**L5401** `03:26:20` defensive tool and an offensive tool.

<a id="L5402"></a>**L5402** `03:26:22` Think of it as a Koshian sword stance on

<a id="L5403"></a>**L5403** `03:26:24` a timer. She can access her ferocious

<a id="L5404"></a>**L5404** `03:26:26` charge by either soft fainting an attack

<a id="L5405"></a>**L5405** `03:26:28` into it or by dodging during most of her

<a id="L5406"></a>**L5406** `03:26:31` recoveries. Nonblockable attacks thrown

<a id="L5407"></a>**L5407** `03:26:33` at her during that run will

<a id="L5408"></a>**L5408** `03:26:35` automatically be deflected with no play

<a id="L5409"></a>**L5409** `03:26:37` input needed. She has access to a very

<a id="L5410"></a>**L5410** `03:26:39` fast light attack of the deflect that

<a id="L5411"></a>**L5411** `03:26:41` allows her to stay safe while

<a id="L5412"></a>**L5412** `03:26:43` outnumbered. She can also use a hyper

<a id="L5413"></a>**L5413** `03:26:45` armored zone attack in order to cleave

<a id="L5414"></a>**L5414** `03:26:47` multiple opponents around her. And

<a id="L5415"></a>**L5415** `03:26:49` lastly, she has a devastating heavy

<a id="L5416"></a>**L5416** `03:26:51` attack that pins an opponent in place

<a id="L5417"></a>**L5417** `03:26:53` and can wall splat. Since fighting

<a id="L5418"></a>**L5418** `03:26:55` against heroes with hyper armor was very

<a id="L5419"></a>**L5419** `03:26:57` problematic due to her lacking a deflect

<a id="L5420"></a>**L5420** `03:26:59` follow-up that can break armor, a later

<a id="L5421"></a>**L5421** `03:27:02` patch allowed her to flow into a kick.

<a id="L5422"></a>**L5422** `03:27:04` This didn't really make her any more

<a id="L5423"></a>**L5423** `03:27:05` oppressive. It just removed many loose-

<a id="L5424"></a>**L5424** `03:27:08` loose situations for her. Coming back to

<a id="L5425"></a>**L5425** `03:27:10` her running stance, out of that stance,

<a id="L5426"></a>**L5426** `03:27:12` she has the ability to throw 400

<a id="L5427"></a>**L5427** `03:27:13` millisecond lights, a 500 millisecond

<a id="L5428"></a>**L5428** `03:27:16` zone attack, and a very similar pinning

<a id="L5429"></a>**L5429** `03:27:18` heavy like her deflect heavy. This pin

<a id="L5430"></a>**L5430** `03:27:21` move led to her being extremely easy to

<a id="L5431"></a>**L5431** `03:27:23` gank with because she could access it so

<a id="L5432"></a>**L5432** `03:27:26` effortlessly. Something that is actually

<a id="L5433"></a>**L5433** `03:27:28` worth noting about this pin move is that

<a id="L5434"></a>**L5434** `03:27:31` the pin could actually confirm three

<a id="L5435"></a>**L5435** `03:27:33` attacks from your teammates since every

<a id="L5436"></a>**L5436** `03:27:35` stage of the animation reapplies hits

<a id="L5437"></a>**L5437** `03:27:38` them and her four feet is absolute BS.

<a id="L5438"></a>**L5438** `03:27:43` >> So the forer North American clan lore

<a id="L5439"></a>**L5439** `03:27:47` goes kind of deep. It's kind of like the

<a id="L5440"></a>**L5440** `03:27:50` story mode of honor but it's actually

<a id="L5441"></a>**L5441** `03:27:52` cool. Lots of clans, communities trying

<a id="L5442"></a>**L5442** `03:27:55` to make something out of Froner back in

<a id="L5443"></a>**L5443** `03:27:57` 2017 and 2018. Call of Duty clan

<a id="L5444"></a>**L5444** `03:28:00` surprisingly came over and tried to do

<a id="L5445"></a>**L5445** `03:28:03` something with it. The biggest clan at

<a id="L5446"></a>**L5446** `03:28:06` the time was Darth Laf and Holy. There

<a id="L5447"></a>**L5447** `03:28:12` was a lot of communities trying to label

<a id="L5448"></a>**L5448** `03:28:14` themselves as clans, but they lacked the

<a id="L5449"></a>**L5449** `03:28:16` GT requirements. They lacked the

<a id="L5450"></a>**L5450** `03:28:18` organization. The Kingdom of Ashefeld

<a id="L5451"></a>**L5451** `03:28:20` was a community I almost joined back in

<a id="L5452"></a>**L5452** `03:28:23` 2018, early 2019. I uh tried looking for

<a id="L5453"></a>**L5453** `03:28:28` a good plan. None of them really stuck,

<a id="L5454"></a>**L5454** `03:28:31` but finally I saw Holy and uh finally

<a id="L5455"></a>**L5455** `03:28:34` decided, sure, I'll join these guys, see

<a id="L5456"></a>**L5456** `03:28:36` how it goes. We played a lot of Fore

<a id="L5457"></a>**L5457** `03:28:39` Honor, hosted a lot of tournaments,

<a id="L5458"></a>**L5458** `03:28:41` hosted a lot of the events and stuff. We

<a id="L5459"></a>**L5459** `03:28:44` had a lot of activity, had a lot of

<a id="L5460"></a>**L5460** `03:28:46` traffic, and uh we did cash prizes many

<a id="L5461"></a>**L5461** `03:28:50` times. We did like a $400 cash prize

<a id="L5462"></a>**L5462** `03:28:54` tournament at some point in 20120. After

<a id="L5463"></a>**L5463** `03:28:57` the years went by, wholly maintained its

<a id="L5464"></a>**L5464** `03:29:01` uh momentum, at least in North America,

<a id="L5465"></a>**L5465** `03:29:03` right? We really just use the Discord,

<a id="L5466"></a>**L5466** `03:29:07` Twitch, other social media apps to keep

<a id="L5467"></a>**L5467** `03:29:10` in contact, organize events and

<a id="L5468"></a>**L5468** `03:29:12` tournaments, games throughout the years.

<a id="L5469"></a>**L5469** `03:29:14` The fact that a lot of us played

<a id="L5470"></a>**L5470** `03:29:17` competitive also helped with a lot of

<a id="L5471"></a>**L5471** `03:29:19` members staying. I would have to say

<a id="L5472"></a>**L5472** `03:29:21` LOF, Exiled, and Darth, they had like

<a id="L5473"></a>**L5473** `03:29:24` about 30 maybe 40 members max at their

<a id="L5474"></a>**L5474** `03:29:28` highest point at one time in For Honor.

<a id="L5475"></a>**L5475** `03:29:30` In Holy, we had 87. We didn't start

<a id="L5476"></a>**L5476** `03:29:33` losing members until like 2024 when it

<a id="L5477"></a>**L5477** `03:29:37` was like clear that for honor was just,

<a id="L5478"></a>**L5478** `03:29:40` you know, exiled. Still walks around a

<a id="L5479"></a>**L5479** `03:29:43` little bit. YL isn't really a thing

<a id="L5480"></a>**L5480** `03:29:46` anymore. And uh LOF eventually got

<a id="L5481"></a>**L5481** `03:29:49` disbanded from internal issues. Darth

<a id="L5482"></a>**L5482** `03:29:52` was kind of the same way. Not really

<a id="L5483"></a>**L5483** `03:29:54` from a leadership perspective, but more

<a id="L5484"></a>**L5484** `03:29:56` like an activity. A lot of people just

<a id="L5485"></a>**L5485** `03:29:58` kind of start branching away from For

<a id="L5486"></a>**L5486** `03:29:59` Honor. Holy. Right now in 2025, we still

<a id="L5487"></a>**L5487** `03:30:03` have about 50 plus members and a lot of

<a id="L5488"></a>**L5488** `03:30:06` them do still play for honor, including

<a id="L5489"></a>**L5489** `03:30:08` myself. We got Holy Jama, which is top

<a id="L5490"></a>**L5490** `03:30:12` five Shagoki on Xbox. Holy Ghost, which

<a id="L5491"></a>**L5491** `03:30:16` is a really good Grandmaster player. I'm

<a id="L5492"></a>**L5492** `03:30:19` number one for Warden, obviously. And we

<a id="L5493"></a>**L5493** `03:30:21` got a couple other people that got

<a id="L5494"></a>**L5494** `03:30:23` really high on Grandmaster leaderboard.

<a id="L5495"></a>**L5495** `03:30:25` Holy Chad got I think it was top five

<a id="L5496"></a>**L5496** `03:30:28` Warden in Australia which was

<a id="L5497"></a>**L5497** `03:30:30` impressive. Holy Elite was also number

<a id="L5498"></a>**L5498** `03:30:33` seven on Xbox for Warden. Fire Priest

<a id="L5499"></a>**L5499** `03:30:36` got up there for Black Prior. We got

<a id="L5500"></a>**L5500** `03:30:38` really sweaty on For Honor. You know,

<a id="L5501"></a>**L5501** `03:30:40` the lore in North American clans does go

<a id="L5502"></a>**L5502** `03:30:43` deeper. Like I can go into like, you

<a id="L5503"></a>**L5503** `03:30:44` know, events, the beefs along the way,

<a id="L5504"></a>**L5504** `03:30:47` the problems that we had, but that's

<a id="L5505"></a>**L5505** `03:30:49` more than the general highlight of what

<a id="L5506"></a>**L5506** `03:30:51` made us stay and what ultimately caused

<a id="L5507"></a>**L5507** `03:30:53` us all to leave. It's just the lack of

<a id="L5508"></a>**L5508** `03:30:56` content, lack of uh activity and care

<a id="L5509"></a>**L5509** `03:30:58` from the devs. But during the first

<a id="L5510"></a>**L5510** `03:31:01` three, four years of the game, it was

<a id="L5511"></a>**L5511** `03:31:03` popping.

<a id="L5512"></a>**L5512** `03:31:06` So, the Fraud YouTube scene has been

<a id="L5513"></a>**L5513** `03:31:09` very sy,

<a id="L5514"></a>**L5514** `03:31:12` especially for the newer content

<a id="L5515"></a>**L5515** `03:31:14` creators. I know the game is kind of in

<a id="L5516"></a>**L5516** `03:31:16` a content drought. So, I highly respect

<a id="L5517"></a>**L5517** `03:31:18` any and all YouTubers like Havoc,

<a id="L5518"></a>**L5518** `03:31:20` Underliner, those guys are like my

<a id="L5519"></a>**L5519** `03:31:21` idols. I've been watching them since I

<a id="L5520"></a>**L5520** `03:31:22` was a kid in high school. When it comes

<a id="L5521"></a>**L5521** `03:31:24` to the people that are trying to break

<a id="L5522"></a>**L5522** `03:31:26` through, I feel like they all try to do

<a id="L5523"></a>**L5523** `03:31:27` the same thing, which is try to paint

<a id="L5524"></a>**L5524** `03:31:29` this game as a toxic cess pit of racism

<a id="L5525"></a>**L5525** `03:31:33` and and hate. when in reality it does

<a id="L5526"></a>**L5526** `03:31:36` exist, don't get me wrong, but it's not

<a id="L5527"></a>**L5527** `03:31:38` to the degree that they they put out

<a id="L5528"></a>**L5528** `03:31:40` there. Some people just have bad games

<a id="L5529"></a>**L5529** `03:31:42` and sometimes they get angry and say

<a id="L5530"></a>**L5530** `03:31:44` some things. Now, they say some really

<a id="L5531"></a>**L5531** `03:31:46` crude things. I think yes, they deserve

<a id="L5532"></a>**L5532** `03:31:48` to be put on blast. But if someone just

<a id="L5533"></a>**L5533** `03:31:50` gets mad at you and says, "Oh, you play

<a id="L5534"></a>**L5534** `03:31:52` really lame. That's really stupid. This

<a id="L5535"></a>**L5535** `03:31:54` character's dumb." That's not them being

<a id="L5536"></a>**L5536** `03:31:56` toxic. That's just them being

<a id="L5537"></a>**L5537** `03:31:57` frustrated. To say to sit there and kind

<a id="L5538"></a>**L5538** `03:32:01` of throw stones at them for something

<a id="L5539"></a>**L5539** `03:32:02` you probably have done yourself really

<a id="L5540"></a>**L5540** `03:32:04` isn't healthy. And I think that just

<a id="L5541"></a>**L5541** `03:32:06` overall you have to remember that

<a id="L5542"></a>**L5542** `03:32:08` YouTube is the main source of

<a id="L5543"></a>**L5543** `03:32:11` advertisement for a game. So when

<a id="L5544"></a>**L5544** `03:32:12` everyone's jumping in and showing that

<a id="L5545"></a>**L5545** `03:32:14` everyone's this mean cesspit of of hate,

<a id="L5546"></a>**L5546** `03:32:18` no one's going to want to play the game.

<a id="L5547"></a>**L5547** `03:32:19` So, I think as a collective, we need to

<a id="L5548"></a>**L5548** `03:32:21` try to push for more positive content

<a id="L5549"></a>**L5549** `03:32:24` instead of putting people on blast for

<a id="L5550"></a>**L5550** `03:32:25` even just the smallest things like rage

<a id="L5551"></a>**L5551** `03:32:27` quitting a game. If a game's not fun,

<a id="L5552"></a>**L5552** `03:32:30` why would you play it?

<a id="L5553"></a>**L5553** `03:32:36` With the Hatun having claimed victory

<a id="L5554"></a>**L5554** `03:32:38` over Horos, the time had come for their

<a id="L5555"></a>**L5555** `03:32:40` gradual takeover of Heathmore. However,

<a id="L5556"></a>**L5556** `03:32:43` a large part of waging war is to deal

<a id="L5557"></a>**L5557** `03:32:45` with the captured enemies of your army

<a id="L5558"></a>**L5558** `03:32:46` as well as entertaining the troops to

<a id="L5559"></a>**L5559** `03:32:48` boost morale. Gulene had a unique method

<a id="L5560"></a>**L5560** `03:32:51` of achieving this. By constructing a

<a id="L5561"></a>**L5561** `03:32:53` coliseum, she would have her prisoners

<a id="L5562"></a>**L5562** `03:32:54` fight and blood sport to the death for

<a id="L5563"></a>**L5563** `03:32:56` the adoration of the crowd and in this

<a id="L5564"></a>**L5564** `03:32:58` way removing prisoners from her care and

<a id="L5565"></a>**L5565** `03:33:00` also lifting the spirits of her

<a id="L5566"></a>**L5566** `03:33:02` soldiers. One such prisoner was the

<a id="L5567"></a>**L5567** `03:33:04` gladiator queen, a defiant and unruly

<a id="L5568"></a>**L5568** `03:33:08` warrior who resisted Gulgene with a

<a id="L5569"></a>**L5569** `03:33:09` passion. Gulgene became so frustrated

<a id="L5570"></a>**L5570** `03:33:12` with her that she often tried to fix

<a id="L5571"></a>**L5571** `03:33:13` matches and cheat to ensure that she

<a id="L5572"></a>**L5572** `03:33:15` would die. Although she always seemed to

<a id="L5573"></a>**L5573** `03:33:17` come out successful no matter the trial.

<a id="L5574"></a>**L5574** `03:33:19` So now Gulgene's new plan is to partner

<a id="L5575"></a>**L5575** `03:33:22` Collapi with her other most notorious

<a id="L5576"></a>**L5576** `03:33:24` prisoner Astria in the hopes that both

<a id="L5577"></a>**L5577** `03:33:27` would fall together. The favor of the

<a id="L5578"></a>**L5578** `03:33:29` crowd is fickle. Lose its favor and you

<a id="L5579"></a>**L5579** `03:33:32` are doomed to fall. But gain it and you

<a id="L5580"></a>**L5580** `03:33:34` shall ascend.

<a id="L5581"></a>**L5581** `03:33:39` Starting in year 9, Ubisoft revamped the

<a id="L5582"></a>**L5582** `03:33:41` menu interface. Now you don't have to

<a id="L5583"></a>**L5583** `03:33:43` open the faction war anymore to select a

<a id="L5584"></a>**L5584** `03:33:45` game mode, pushing the faction war away

<a id="L5585"></a>**L5585** `03:33:47` to its irrelevancy like it deserves as

<a id="L5586"></a>**L5586** `03:33:49` the game has far progressed away from

<a id="L5587"></a>**L5587** `03:33:51` the whole knights Vikings samurai. And

<a id="L5588"></a>**L5588** `03:33:54` we also saw some minor changes for

<a id="L5589"></a>**L5589** `03:33:55` several heroes. Armusha's heavy finishes

<a id="L5590"></a>**L5590** `03:33:58` are now unblockable. How it start

<a id="L5591"></a>**L5591** `03:33:59` recovery got nerfed, so it can chain his

<a id="L5592"></a>**L5592** `03:34:01` lights into heavies now, something that

<a id="L5593"></a>**L5593** `03:34:03` he couldn't do on release for some

<a id="L5594"></a>**L5594** `03:34:04` reason. And most importantly, Viji's

<a id="L5595"></a>**L5595** `03:34:07` lights are no longer enhanced, kicking

<a id="L5596"></a>**L5596** `03:34:09` her out from the top three in duels.

<a id="L5597"></a>**L5597** `03:34:12` This season, we saw a 2v2v2 gladiator

<a id="L5598"></a>**L5598** `03:34:14` arena event be released. It's an

<a id="L5599"></a>**L5599** `03:34:17` elimination game on the Viking Arena

<a id="L5603"></a>**L5603** `03:34:24` stays away and kills whatever team won

<a id="L5604"></a>**L5604** `03:34:25` their 2v2. It was fun, however, even I

<a id="L5605"></a>**L5605** `03:34:28` played it. And the gladiator skin was

<a id="L5606"></a>**L5606** `03:34:29` pretty cool, too.
