<a id="L5773"></a>**L5773** `03:40:18` unfortunately has a controversial style

<a id="L5774"></a>**L5774** `03:40:20` of gameplay because she has a stance

<a id="L5775"></a>**L5775** `03:40:22` where she is immune to pretty much all

<a id="L5776"></a>**L5776** `03:40:24` attacks except undgeables and guard

<a id="L5777"></a>**L5777** `03:40:27` breaks of course. And people don't like

<a id="L5778"></a>**L5778** `03:40:29` that. People don't like the ability to

<a id="L5779"></a>**L5779** `03:40:31` essentially be invincible just by

<a id="L5780"></a>**L5780** `03:40:33` standing there. you're you're forced to

<a id="L5781"></a>**L5781** `03:40:35` play her game if she gets in the stance

<a id="L5782"></a>**L5782** `03:40:38` because she does a lot of damage and

<a id="L5783"></a>**L5783** `03:40:40` it's it's hard to actually hit her. If

<a id="L5784"></a>**L5784** `03:40:42` you guess wrong, you eat a lot of damage

<a id="L5785"></a>**L5785** `03:40:43` in most cases. Playing her, she is

<a id="L5786"></a>**L5786** `03:40:46` extremely fun. They gave her three

<a id="L5787"></a>**L5787** `03:40:48` stances. One was a dual stance, one was

<a id="L5788"></a>**L5788** `03:40:50` like a team fighting stance, and one

<a id="L5789"></a>**L5789** `03:40:52` stance was for ganks, and it was top

<a id="L5790"></a>**L5790** `03:40:54` side, right side, and left side if you

<a id="L5791"></a>**L5791** `03:40:55` were in your stance. The top part gave

<a id="L5792"></a>**L5792** `03:40:57` you unblockable heavies, as well as

<a id="L5793"></a>**L5793** `03:40:58` quicker lights. The right side stance

<a id="L5794"></a>**L5794** `03:41:01` gave you hyper armor heavies and

<a id="L5795"></a>**L5795** `03:41:03` crushing counter lights. And the left

<a id="L5796"></a>**L5796** `03:41:04` side gave you really high damage

<a id="L5797"></a>**L5797** `03:41:06` heavies, poison lights, and a bash that

<a id="L5798"></a>**L5798** `03:41:10` was like a pin that did damage. Pretty

<a id="L5799"></a>**L5799** `03:41:12` sick design. Similar to a fear on

<a id="L5800"></a>**L5800** `03:41:14` release. While Afiras I felt like were

<a id="L5801"></a>**L5801** `03:41:16` more just different forms of offense,

<a id="L5802"></a>**L5802** `03:41:18` this just seemed to be more of different

<a id="L5803"></a>**L5803** `03:41:20` types of fighting styles. But at the end

<a id="L5804"></a>**L5804** `03:41:22` of the day, she still has that problem

<a id="L5805"></a>**L5805** `03:41:24` where she can essentially babysit gank,

<a id="L5806"></a>**L5806** `03:41:27` as the community calls it, where if

<a id="L5807"></a>**L5807** `03:41:28` you're in a two versus one, she can just

<a id="L5808"></a>**L5808** `03:41:31` choose to interrupt with really quick

<a id="L5809"></a>**L5809** `03:41:32` attacks. If the enemy does anything to

<a id="L5810"></a>**L5810** `03:41:34` attack your teammate, you can usually

<a id="L5811"></a>**L5811** `03:41:36` pull it out and punish them for

<a id="L5812"></a>**L5812** `03:41:38` attacking your teammate. If they try to

<a id="L5813"></a>**L5813** `03:41:40` attack you while you're in that stance,

<a id="L5814"></a>**L5814** `03:41:42` she can do her counter. But if they try

<a id="L5815"></a>**L5815** `03:41:43` to guard break her, then your teammate

<a id="L5816"></a>**L5816** `03:41:45` can save you pretty easily. So she is

<a id="L5817"></a>**L5817** `03:41:48` dangerous in a 2v1 or if there are

<a id="L5818"></a>**L5818** `03:41:51` multiple virtuosas. If there's two

<a id="L5819"></a>**L5819** `03:41:53` versus you, it is an almost unwinable

<a id="L5820"></a>**L5820** `03:41:56` fight without some sort of undgeable.

<a id="L5821"></a>**L5821** `03:42:01` After years and years of Ubisoft

<a id="L5822"></a>**L5822** `03:42:03` removing stamina damage from For Honor,

<a id="L5823"></a>**L5823** `03:42:05` this season saw the final nail in the

<a id="L5824"></a>**L5824** `03:42:07` coffin for the last stamina bullies left

<a id="L5825"></a>**L5825** `03:42:10` as all stamina damage outside of carb

<a id="L5826"></a>**L5826** `03:42:13` break throw was removed from the game.

<a id="L5827"></a>**L5827** `03:42:15` This was a massive blow, especially for

<a id="L5828"></a>**L5828** `03:42:17` Black Prior, who's seen everything that

<a id="L5829"></a>**L5829** `03:42:19` made him viable be systematically

<a id="L5830"></a>**L5830** `03:42:21` removed from the game over the years. We

<a id="L5831"></a>**L5831** `03:42:23` also saw a massive buff to pirate,

<a id="L5832"></a>**L5832** `03:42:25` probably to celebrate her second skin,

<a id="L5833"></a>**L5833** `03:42:27` which makes her look like an actual

<a id="L5834"></a>**L5834** `03:42:29` stereotypical pirate. Her unblockable

<a id="L5835"></a>**L5835** `03:42:31` soft faint to guar.

<a id="L5836"></a>**L5836** `03:42:36` But what that did was make the attack

<a id="L5837"></a>**L5837** `03:42:38` unreactable on flash on PC, which is a

<a id="L5838"></a>**L5838** `03:42:41` massive thing. kicking her from the

<a id="L5839"></a>**L5839** `03:42:43` bottom tier for PC players to the best

<a id="L5840"></a>**L5840** `03:42:47` dualist in the entire game with a simple

<a id="L5841"></a>**L5841** `03:42:50` 33 millisecond change on her

<a id="L5842"></a>**L5842** `03:42:52` unblockable. Besides that, we saw some

<a id="L5843"></a>**L5843** `03:42:54` more minor changes. Some bashes no

<a id="L5844"></a>**L5844** `03:42:56` longer chain on roof anymore, a lot of

<a id="L5845"></a>**L5845** `03:42:58` bug fixes, and some attacks becoming a

<a id="L5846"></a>**L5846** `03:43:00` few milliseconds faster, but nothing

<a id="L5847"></a>**L5847** `03:43:02` really noteworthy.

<a id="L5848"></a>**L5848** `03:43:08` If you really think about it, the

<a id="L5849"></a>**L5849** `03:43:10` deciding factor that allowed for Honor

<a id="L5850"></a>**L5850** `03:43:12` to not only compete, but also thrive in

<a id="L5851"></a>**L5851** `03:43:15` a market that is heavily dominated by

<a id="L5852"></a>**L5852** `03:43:18` other wellestablished studios is its

<a id="L5853"></a>**L5853** `03:43:21` stance widget system. This game puts a

<a id="L5854"></a>**L5854** `03:43:23` lot of emphasis on reactions and their

<a id="L5855"></a>**L5855** `03:43:26` ability to gain and maintain momentum.

<a id="L5856"></a>**L5856** `03:43:29` The depth for Honor reaches with its 38

<a id="L5857"></a>**L5857** `03:43:32` unique heroes is just unimaginable. From

<a id="L5858"></a>**L5858** `03:43:35` situationally specific punishes to

<a id="L5859"></a>**L5859** `03:43:37` matchup specific counters, the list of

<a id="L5860"></a>**L5860** `03:43:40` things to master when it comes to combat

<a id="L5861"></a>**L5861** `03:43:42` is never ending. Another aspect that

<a id="L5862"></a>**L5862** `03:43:44` differentiates For Honor from other

<a id="L5863"></a>**L5863** `03:43:46` fighting games is the ability to face

<a id="L5864"></a>**L5864** `03:43:49` off against more than one opponent while

<a id="L5865"></a>**L5865** `03:43:51` still maintaining the core concept of

<a id="L5866"></a>**L5866** `03:43:53` combat. With hero specific feats also

<a id="L5867"></a>**L5867** `03:43:56` being sprinkled into the chaos of 4v4

<a id="L5868"></a>**L5868** `03:43:58` game modes, there are numerous moments

<a id="L5869"></a>**L5869** `03:44:01` that happen seamlessly in the flow of a

<a id="L5870"></a>**L5870** `03:44:03` match which can't be compared to any

<a id="L5871"></a>**L5871** `03:44:06` other game. The feeling of coming out

<a id="L5872"></a>**L5872** `03:44:08` alive when outnumbered by multiple

<a id="L5873"></a>**L5873** `03:44:10` opponents is just bliss. The feeling of

<a id="L5874"></a>**L5874** `03:44:13` performing the perfect gank also can't

<a id="L5875"></a>**L5875** `03:44:15` be compared. For Honor also nails the

<a id="L5876"></a>**L5876** `03:44:18` setting. How much creative freedom you

<a id="L5877"></a>**L5877** `03:44:20` have when it comes to looks and

<a id="L5878"></a>**L5878** `03:44:22` executions truly elevates the game above

<a id="L5879"></a>**L5879** `03:44:26` any of its competitors, if not even most

<a id="L5880"></a>**L5880** `03:44:29` games in general. Forun is a game that

<a id="L5881"></a>**L5881** `03:44:31` creates a feeling while playing that

<a id="L5882"></a>**L5882** `03:44:33` just can't be mimicked by any other.

<a id="L5883"></a>**L5883** `03:44:36` Sound design, optics, and gameplay flow

<a id="L5884"></a>**L5884** `03:44:39` perfectly into each other, making for

<a id="L5885"></a>**L5885** `03:44:41` Honor into a truly unique game.

<a id="L5886"></a>**L5886** `03:44:47` Long ago, when the samurai and the Mayer

<a id="L5887"></a>**L5887** `03:44:50` were still young and coming into their

<a id="L5888"></a>**L5888** `03:44:51` new home, there was a fortress known as

<a id="L5889"></a>**L5889** `03:44:53` Castle Chio, a place of great importance

<a id="L5890"></a>**L5890** `03:44:56` to the samurai, as it carried the legacy

<a id="L5891"></a>**L5891** `03:44:58` of their people. But where greatness

<a id="L5892"></a>**L5892** `03:45:00` dwells, there will be those who seek to

<a id="L5893"></a>**L5893** `03:45:01` claim it. While its damio was away, the

<a id="L5894"></a>**L5894** `03:45:04` castle was besieged by Katsuo, the

<a id="L5895"></a>**L5895** `03:45:06` greedy damio, seeking more power for

<a id="L5896"></a>**L5896** `03:45:08` himself. Lady Sauri, the wife of the

<a id="L5897"></a>**L5897** `03:45:10` Chio's Daimo, tried to rally a defense,

<a id="L5898"></a>**L5898** `03:45:13` but was overwhelmed and defeated. But

<a id="L5899"></a>**L5899** `03:45:15` not before a darkness awoke within her,

<a id="L5900"></a>**L5900** `03:45:17` a powerful and ancient evil known as the

<a id="L5901"></a>**L5901** `03:45:20` Jorumo. Giving in to the darkness, Sauri

<a id="L5902"></a>**L5902** `03:45:23` became the mother of Yo-kai and

<a id="L5903"></a>**L5903** `03:45:25` destroyed Katsurro's army, spreading her

<a id="L5904"></a>**L5904** `03:45:27` taint across the Mire. Thankfully, the

<a id="L5905"></a>**L5905** `03:45:29` return of her son, Yoshiro, along with

<a id="L5906"></a>**L5906** `03:45:32` his family heirloom, the Mimono dagger,

<a id="L5907"></a>**L5907** `03:45:34` proved to be the necessary salvation

<a id="L5908"></a>**L5908** `03:45:36` their land needed. Wielding the dagger,

<a id="L5909"></a>**L5909** `03:45:38` Yoshro slay every Yo-kai he met,

<a id="L5910"></a>**L5910** `03:45:40` including his own mother, in order to

<a id="L5911"></a>**L5911** `03:45:42` save the Meyer. He managed to seal her

<a id="L5912"></a>**L5912** `03:45:44` in a cave, blocked away by special seals

<a id="L5913"></a>**L5913** `03:45:47` to make sure that she never got loose.

<a id="L5914"></a>**L5914** `03:45:49` Meanwhile, he was forced to deal with

<a id="L5915"></a>**L5915** `03:45:51` awful corruption, a price paid for the

<a id="L5916"></a>**L5916** `03:45:54` use of the dagger. Though this story is

<a id="L5917"></a>**L5917** `03:45:56` from the ancient past, its meanings

<a id="L5918"></a>**L5918** `03:45:58` resonate in the here and now on how

<a id="L5919"></a>**L5919** `03:46:00` pride, vanity, and greed are all a man's

<a id="L5920"></a>**L5920** `03:46:04` downfall.

<a id="L5921"></a>**L5921** `03:46:12` As a storyteller and a writer, the lore

<a id="L5922"></a>**L5922** `03:46:14` of For Honor is what really captivated

<a id="L5923"></a>**L5923** `03:46:16` me. I love history and I love stories,

<a id="L5924"></a>**L5924** `03:46:19` so getting to explore why these three

<a id="L5925"></a>**L5925** `03:46:20` factions, the knights, the samurai, and

<a id="L5926"></a>**L5926** `03:46:22` the Vikings were fighting was what

<a id="L5927"></a>**L5927** `03:46:24` really sold me. I know for many, lore

<a id="L5928"></a>**L5928** `03:46:26` isn't all that important. But I will

<a id="L5929"></a>**L5929** `03:46:28` always attest that without a reason to

<a id="L5930"></a>**L5930** `03:46:30` fight, the game would not be as

<a id="L5931"></a>**L5931** `03:46:31` engrossing. That isn't to say the lore

<a id="L5932"></a>**L5932** `03:46:33` has always been good. It has moments of

<a id="L5933"></a>**L5933** `03:46:36` brilliance and moments of mediocrity,

<a id="L5934"></a>**L5934** `03:46:38` and I'm woe to confess I've seen far

<a id="L5935"></a>**L5935** `03:46:40` more of the latter than the former as of

<a id="L5936"></a>**L5936** `03:46:41` late. I personally believe that year 3

<a id="L5937"></a>**L5937** `03:46:44` was perhaps the true epitome of strong

<a id="L5938"></a>**L5938** `03:46:46` lore from For Honor, showing such a

<a id="L5939"></a>**L5939** `03:46:48` clever form of cause and effect. How

<a id="L5940"></a>**L5940** `03:46:49` each season a new character and event

<a id="L5941"></a>**L5941** `03:46:51` flowed into the other, creating a domino

<a id="L5942"></a>**L5942** `03:46:53` effect of events, characters, and

<a id="L5943"></a>**L5943** `03:46:55` stories. But on the other hand, the

<a id="L5944"></a>**L5944** `03:46:57` inclusion of some of the Outlanders like

<a id="L5945"></a>**L5945** `03:46:59` Aira and Ocelot all felt disgustingly

<a id="L5946"></a>**L5946** `03:47:01` similar to me. repetitive instances of

<a id="L5947"></a>**L5947** `03:47:04` Horcos doing something evil to an

<a id="L5948"></a>**L5948** `03:47:05` outside culture only for that culture to

<a id="L5949"></a>**L5949** `03:47:07` arrive for revenge but not cause any

<a id="L5950"></a>**L5950** `03:47:09` meaningful change or impact on Horcos

<a id="L5951"></a>**L5951** `03:47:11` outside of the arrival of a new hero.

<a id="L5952"></a>**L5952** `03:47:14` There didn't seem to be any stakes or

<a id="L5953"></a>**L5953** `03:47:15` weight or impact during those seasons

<a id="L5954"></a>**L5954** `03:47:17` and it was felt by myself and many

<a id="L5955"></a>**L5955** `03:47:19` others who appreciate lore in writing.

<a id="L5956"></a>**L5956** `03:47:22` Look, I'm a storyteller before I'm a

<a id="L5957"></a>**L5957** `03:47:24` gamer. I make a hobby out of telling my

<a id="L5958"></a>**L5958** `03:47:26` own stories within the world of

<a id="L5959"></a>**L5959** `03:47:27` Heathmore. And I don't say this to brag,

<a id="L5960"></a>**L5960** `03:47:29` but rather to make a point. The world of

<a id="L5961"></a>**L5961** `03:47:31` For Honor is a perfect setting to create

<a id="L5962"></a>**L5962** `03:47:34` incredible stories on the macro or micro

<a id="L5963"></a>**L5963** `03:47:37` level. The lore of For Honor can be as

<a id="L5964"></a>**L5964** `03:47:39` rich and as beautiful as the writers are

<a id="L5965"></a>**L5965** `03:47:41` prepared to reach. I want to see this

<a id="L5966"></a>**L5966** `03:47:44` lore reach the stars, and I pray that it

<a id="L5967"></a>**L5967** `03:47:46` will because it's too good a setting to

<a id="L5968"></a>**L5968** `03:47:48` let it fall by the wayside. But that's

<a id="L5969"></a>**L5969** `03:47:51` just my thought on the matter. My name

<a id="L5970"></a>**L5970** `03:47:53` is Raven Knight, and that was the lore

<a id="L5971"></a>**L5971** `03:47:55` of For Honor. Thanks for listening.

<a id="L5972"></a>**L5972** `03:48:03` This season, Yan Gunner's damage got

<a id="L5973"></a>**L5973** `03:48:05` slightly buffed, revenge got slightly

<a id="L5974"></a>**L5974** `03:48:07` changed, Conqueror finally got a wall

<a id="L5975"></a>**L5975** `03:48:08` catcher, and the Spider event came back

<a id="L5976"></a>**L5976** `03:48:11` together with an Nushi skin.

<a id="L5977"></a>**L5977** `03:48:15` And that's it. We have reached the

<a id="L5978"></a>**L5978** `03:48:18` current season as of time of recording,

<a id="L5979"></a>**L5979** `03:48:20` year 9, season 3, the 35th season of For

<a id="L5980"></a>**L5980** `03:48:25` Honor. Ever since release, people have

<a id="L5981"></a>**L5981** `03:48:28` been talking about how the game is

<a id="L5982"></a>**L5982** `03:48:29` dying. I knew for Honor Killer was

<a id="L5983"></a>**L5983** `03:48:32` popping out pretty much every year now.

<a id="L5984"></a>**L5984** `03:48:34` And yet, almost a decade later, the game

<a id="L5985"></a>**L5985** `03:48:36` is still alive with regular content, a

<a id="L5986"></a>**L5986** `03:48:39` tight-knitted community, and still new

<a id="L5987"></a>**L5987** `03:48:41` players joining and learning this game.

<a id="L5988"></a>**L5988** `03:48:43` The reason Foruna refused to die is

<a id="L5989"></a>**L5989** `03:48:45` simply that there is no alternative to

<a id="L5990"></a>**L5990** `03:48:47` it. Many games tried, but nothing gets

<a id="L5991"></a>**L5991** `03:48:49` close to the combat style that For Honor

<a id="L5992"></a>**L5992** `03:48:51` has. In the end, despite everything

<a id="L5993"></a>**L5993** `03:48:53` that's happened to For Honor with games

<a id="L5994"></a>**L5994** `03:48:56` releasing like Elden Ring, a lot of

<a id="L5995"></a>**L5995** `03:48:58` people thought that we killed the game

<a id="L5996"></a>**L5996** `03:48:59` more or less, Froner's went through a

<a id="L5997"></a>**L5997** `03:49:03` lot uh a lot of ups and downs. I feel

<a id="L5998"></a>**L5998** `03:49:05` like it's mainly downs nowadays, but

<a id="L5999"></a>**L5999** `03:49:07` either way, Froner will always have a

<a id="L6000"></a>**L6000** `03:49:09` fan base that is dedicated to it. And

<a id="L6001"></a>**L6001** `03:49:12` Ubisoft will hopefully continue to

<a id="L6002"></a>**L6002** `03:49:15` provide updates as long as it has such

<a id="L6003"></a>**L6003** `03:49:18` an active community as it does now. And

<a id="L6004"></a>**L6004** `03:49:20` I highly doubt any game will be able to

<a id="L6005"></a>**L6005** `03:49:23` kill it unless they release A for Honor

<a id="L6006"></a>**L6006** `03:49:25` 2 that that would kill this For Honor.

<a id="L6007"></a>**L6007** `03:49:31` This game I do see potentially making a

<a id="L6008"></a>**L6008** `03:49:34` second game. I don't think this game

<a id="L6009"></a>**L6009** `03:49:37` specifically just for Honor 1 will last

<a id="L6010"></a>**L6010** `03:49:39` past 10 years. I think 10 years will be

<a id="L6011"></a>**L6011** `03:49:43` the mark and then they'll probably

<a id="L6012"></a>**L6012** `03:49:44` announce or they'll probably announce

<a id="L6013"></a>**L6013** `03:49:46` sometime soon that they're going to make

<a id="L6014"></a>**L6014** `03:49:47` a second game and start either from the

<a id="L6015"></a>**L6015** `03:49:50` ground up or just make a complete

<a id="L6016"></a>**L6016** `03:49:51` revamped version of this game cuz this

<a id="L6017"></a>**L6017** `03:49:54` game did not perform bad. Like everyone

<a id="L6018"></a>**L6018** `03:49:56` over the years is like, "Oh, Ferrar's

<a id="L6019"></a>**L6019** `03:49:57` going to die. Fern's going to die." I

<a id="L6020"></a>**L6020** `03:49:59` mean, people been saying Fran's going to

<a id="L6021"></a>**L6021** `03:50:00` die like for 9 years straight. It's like

<a id="L6022"></a>**L6022** `03:50:02` we're in year 9 and this game still

<a id="L6023"></a>**L6023** `03:50:04` isn't dead yet. I mean, it's not the

<a id="L6024"></a>**L6024** `03:50:06` giant player base that like Call of Duty
